### Organisational Obligations (Open)

| ID | Status | Evidence or Gap |
|---|---|---|
| CMP-03 Legal basis for account data (explicit consent per PLAN) | decision | **Decision:** PLAN's stated legal basis (explicit consent) is not what is built for accounts (notice plus age box). Whether a separate consent box is needed or the basis should change is a legal judgement. Evidence: PLAN.md:1085 names "persetujuan eksplisit untuk akun pelapor" as the basis for account processing. Account creation records only the 18+ statement (declareAge server/auth.server.ts:231-247, called from databaseHooks.user.create.before :595-598, users.age_declared_at and age_consent_version), no consent record for processing name, email and WhatsApp number; the Daftar copy is informational (messages/id.json:1852 signin_up_intro) plus a /privasi link (app/routes/account/sign-in.tsx:212). An account that only follows issues or rates has no recorded processing consent. Next step: User and legal reviewer decide (PLAN §14 item 12 legal text review). If consent is kept as the basis: backend adds users.account_consent_version and account_consented_at set in the same databaseHooks.user.create.before as declareAge, refuses creation without it (TDD in server/auth.test.ts like "without the box: no user"); the box itself is frontend. |
| CMP-09 Retention of account data, reports, issues, complaints | decision | **Decision:** No retention period exists for these categories, so none can be enforced. Evidence: PLAN.md:1100 "data akun (nama, email, nomor WhatsApp), laporan dan isu, serta aduan konten: belum diputuskan (§14 butir 29)". Pasal 16(2)(g) quoted from pasal.id/peraturan/uu/uu-no-27-tahun-2022/pasal-16: "Data Pribadi dimusnahkan dan/atau dihapus setelah masa retensi berakhir". worker/maintenance.ts has no job for users, reports, content_complaints or redaction_originals. Next step: Open PLAN §14 item 29 for the user. Once decided, backend adds the deletion to maintenance-daily with a test per category (pattern of "rejected originals go after 90 days, newer stay"). |
| CMP-11 PP 33/2026 breach articles and interim recipient | decision | **Decision:** Recipient before the Lembaga PDP exists and the PP 33/2026 text are unconfirmed. Evidence: PLAN.md:1077 cites PP 33/2026 Pasal 114 (written notice 3×24 jam to subjects and Lembaga) and Pasal 115 (public notice); PLAN.md:1076 says the text came from an unofficial copy. Source unofficial, user to confirm. PLAN.md:1122 lists the interim recipient as open. Next step: User and legal reviewer confirm the recipient and the official PP 33/2026 text (PLAN §11 open items) and the runbook from CMP-10 is updated. |
| CMP-14 Sufficiency of self-declared age (PP 33/2026 Pasal 38, PP 17/2025 Pasal 2) | decision | **Decision:** Whether a self-declaration satisfies the child-identification duty is open. Evidence: PLAN.md:1081 (PP 33/2026 Pasal 38, source unofficial, user to confirm), PLAN.md:1115-1117 (PP 17/2025 Pasal 2 and 4(2), adjustment until 27 Mar 2027 per Pasal 49), PLAN.md:1125 and §14 item 8 (spike P22). Next step: User and legal reviewer decide (spike P22). Minimum-age information on /privasi and /pedoman is frontend text. |
| CMP-19 Urgent takedown orders outside working hours | decision | **Decision:** No guaranteed responder for a 4 h order at night. Evidence: PLAN.md:1245 §14 item 9; docs/runbook.md:198-200 on-call is "best effort". Technical path is immediate (CMP-17) but needs a human to act on the order. Next step: Legal entity decides on-call coverage (PLAN §14 item 9). |
| CMP-21 PSE Lingkup Privat registration (Permenkominfo 5/2020 Pasal 2(1), 7(2)) | decision | **Decision:** Registration not done; access blocking is the stated sanction. Evidence: PLAN.md:1071-1074 and :1198 "TDPSE terbit. [di luar kode]"; needs a legal entity and NIB (PLAN.md:27, :1060). Next step: Open organisational obligation for the legal entity (PLAN §11 item 2, §14 item 1). Never marked done here. |
| CMP-24 Scope of CC BY over third-party content in issue JSON | decision | **Decision:** Whether the operator may license reporters' evidence images and quoted agency text as CC BY 4.0 is unresolved. Evidence: Issue JSON meta.license CC-BY-4.0 covers the whole payload server/api/public-data.ts:284-345, which includes redacted evidence media URLs (reporters' photos), agency responses and referrals text. The step 7 consent (shared/report-fields.ts:69-75, PLAN.md:551) permits use for a public issue, not relicensing reporters' photos under CC BY. Next step: Legal reviewer decides. If excluded, backend adds meta.licenseExceptions naming evidence media and quoted responses (one test), and docs/architecture.md Public Data API states it. |
| CMP-30 GPL components inside published container images | decision | **Decision:** Distributing public images with GPL binaries brings source-availability duties; image visibility and the notice approach are undecided. Evidence: PLAN.md:1004 and :1018 push images to GHCR "gratis untuk image publik" (.github/workflows/deploy.yml:61-62 app and postgres images). `docker run --rm --entrypoint sh sadar-bersuara:local -c 'ffmpeg -buildconf \| grep enable-gpl; grep License: /usr/share/doc/<pkg>/copyright'` output: "enable-gpl", "poppler-utils: License: GPL-2 or GPL-3", "libde265-0: License: LGPL-3+", "libheif1: License: LGPL-3+"; sadar-bersuara-postgres:local PostGIS copyright "License: GPL-2+". infra/Dockerfile:9-28 pins the Debian snapshot, which locates corresponding sources. Next step: User decides with PLAN §14 item 4: keep GHCR images private, or publish a NOTICE with the Debian snapshot id and upstream source links (infra/README.md) for each GPL or LGPL package. |
| CMP-31 Licence of the project's own code | decision | **Decision:** Code licence undecided. Evidence: PLAN.md:1240 §14 item 4 (AGPL-3.0 or MIT/Apache-2.0) open; repo has no LICENSE file (`ls \| grep -i -E "licen\|notice\|third"` empty) and package.json has no license field. Next step: User decides PLAN §14 item 4, then add LICENSE and the package.json license field. |
| CMP-32 Legal entity, DPIA and DPO (UU PDP Pasal 34, 53) | decision | **Decision:** No legal entity, DPIA or DPO yet. Evidence: PLAN.md:57 (critique 4), :1060, :1088-1089, :1163 "[di luar kode]"; PP 33/2026 Pasal 120-122 and 142-147 at PLAN.md:1078-1079, source unofficial, user to confirm. Runbook steps depend on a DPO (docs/runbook.md:111, :130). Next step: Open organisational obligations (PLAN §11, §14 item 1). Never marked done. |
| CMP-33 Cross-border transfer to foreign subprocessors (UU PDP Pasal 56) | decision | **Decision:** Transfer basis not assessed. Evidence: PLAN.md:1101 and :1126 (Brevo, Amazon SES, Cloudflare incl. R2, Backblaze B2, WhatsApp/Meta; spike P22). PP 33/2026 Pasal 160-174 at PLAN.md:1080, source unofficial, user to confirm. Next step: User and legal reviewer decide per subprocessor (spike P22). |

## Performance

Budgets and measured values live in [Performance Budgets](architecture.md#performance-budgets) and the Starting Values table in docs/architecture.md. Load numbers so far are **local only** (workflow 9, 21 October 2026, WSL2 laptop, production compose and images, sample seed, k6 2.3.0, no Cloudflare): 10 minutes at 30.0 req/s, 18,001 SSR requests, 0 failed, p95 40.7 ms, p99 54.9 ms, slowest page `/data` p95 51.8 ms and p99 72.4 ms, uploads 4 at once with the rest answered 503 and retried. The origin run through Access, the 1-hour soak and the mid-run purge wait for a host (PERF-23).

Data classes and their protection rules are in [Data Classification](architecture.md#data-classification); logs and their readers in [Log Inventory](architecture.md#log-inventory).

| PRIV-21 | pass | report_contacts is restricted to admins by a restrictive staff_only policy (db/schema/reports.ts:195-199) on top of FORCE RLS; revealContact audits every view as report.view_contact (app/features/moderation/contact.server.ts; tests app/features/moderation/contact.test.ts:65 'a admin reads it, every view audited', :81 'reporters, other campuses and no contact read nothing'; db/tenant.test.ts:380 describe('report_contacts')). audit_log stores HMAC value hashes, never free text, and never addresses or phone numbers (docs/architecture.md:187). |
| PRIV-22 | na | Retired 2026-10-25: the tuntutan ranking replaced the rating and names no one, so named raters were removed (docs/decisions.md ADR-25). |
| PRIV-23 | na | Retired 2026-10-25: the tuntutan ranking replaced the rating and names no one, so named raters were removed (docs/decisions.md ADR-25). |
| PRIV-24 | pass | Backups and restores: deleted or redacted data stays in backups for a stated period (pgBackRest about 2 to 3 weeks, B2 copy about 8 weeks, restic 3 months) and the privacy policy is to publish it (docs/architecture.md:946, PLAN.md section 11 item 3). Backups are client-side encrypted (pgBackRest aes-256-cbc, docs/architecture.md:938). A single-tenant restore prints post-target account closures that a person must re-apply (docs/architecture.md:923, infra/tenant-load.sh refuses without --confirm). |
| PRIV-25 | decision | **Decision:** Organisational privacy obligations are open. Evidence: PLAN.md section 11 item 3 and 'Masih Perlu Konfirmasi Ahli Hukum': DPIA (UU PDP Pasal 34) and DPO (UU PDP Pasal 53) required, cross-border basis for Brevo, Amazon SES, Cloudflare and R2, Backblaze B2 and WhatsApp (UU PDP Pasal 56, spike P22), breach notice 3x24 jam (PP 33/2026 Pasal 114, source unofficial, user to confirm), self-declared 18+ sufficiency (PP 33/2026 Pasal 38, source unofficial, user to confirm; PP 17/2025 Pasal 2). Next step: User and legal counsel (PLAN section 11 and section 14); never marked done by engineering. |

## Frontend Handoff

| V14.3.3 | V14 | Browser storage holds sensitive report content (story text, location, relation), not only session tokens. The control lives in app/ UI (teammate). Either keep the draft in memory only and accept losing it on reload, or keep it in sessionStorage with a TTL (e.g. drop a draft older than 2 h on read) and record the accepted risk in docs/decisions.md. Backend support: the optional Clear-Site-Data on sign-out (V14.3.1). CVSS 4.0 Low (AV:P/AC:L/AT:P/PR:N/UI:P/VC:L): needs physical access to an unlocked shared device with the same tab still open. |

Backend changes waiting for UI work:

- `addPhone` takes the session id as its fourth argument (`session.session.id`), so a stolen old session cannot add a number (V7.5.1).
- "Sesi Aktif" page with `listSessions` and `revokeSessions` (app/features/report/sessions.server.ts), and "Keluarkan sesi lain" offered after a number is added or TOTP is enrolled; `{ ok: false, reason: "stale" }` from `revokeSessions` sends the user to /masuk (V7.4.3, V7.5.2; contract in docs/architecture.md "Session list (frontend contract)").
- Backup codes are now 20 characters (xxxxx-xxxxx-xxxxx-xxxxx); the message keys `twofactor_backup_hint` and `error_backup_format` still describe 10 (V6.5.2).
- Reporter-facing TOTP offer and WhatsApp risk text (recycled numbers, SIM swap) (V6.3.3, V6.6.1).
- Admin tuntutan page: show `ManagedDemand.prefixAnomaly`, and an Indonesian message for the 429 `prefix_cap` from `rankDemands` (V2.3.2).
- The step-5 evidence picker refuses a file whose extension disagrees with its type (`error_evidence_type`) (V5.2.2).
- /lacak button for `withdrawForwardConsent` (CMP-07).
- /kelola/tuntutan/:code: a legal takedown panel calling `legalTakedownDemand` (CMP-18).
- Labels and `AUDIT_ACTIONS` entries for `report.consent_withdraw`, `account.delete`; `komdigi_order` and `data_subject_request` labels wherever a tuntutan's `hiddenReason` is shown.
- /privasi retention lines for the session user agent (30 days) and invitation addresses (30 days after expiry) (PRIV-08, PRIV-19).
- "Unduh data yang ditampilkan" may link `/api/v1/data/README.txt` (CMP-23).
- Survey, claim rating and right-of-reply routes do not exist yet; the four e2e/api.e2e.ts scenarios for them are `test.fixme` until the routes ship (docs/architecture.md "Resolution Claims, Survey and Right of Reply").

**Dev database incident (audit process, not a product defect, resolved 22 October 2026).** During the audit (21 October 2026, 15:12 WIB) one test run inherited .env, so `bun test` ran with NODE_ENV=development and db/client.server.ts:152 `appDatabaseUrl()` picked DATABASE_URL: the run wrote to the dev database `sadar_bersuara`. Measured read-only against a copy taken at 15:05 (per-table content md5 over every public table): only audit_log, notifications and pgboss.job differ. Added rows: 24 notifications (template referral_deadline, 12 per campus), 8 audit_log rows `referral.remind`, 24 pgboss.job rows `email-send` in state created (created_on 2026-10-21 08:12:05Z). No UPDATE or DELETE reached the dev database. The runner was fixed (NODE_ENV=test and every DATABASE\_\* URL pointed at the test database) and a rerun left the dev database byte-identical. A second run on 22 October 2026 (wf12 coverage probe, same cause) only recomputed the derived dashboard_snapshots and national_aggregates. Resolved on 22 October 2026 by option A: the user rebuilt the dev database (`docker compose -f infra/compose.dev.yaml down -v`, `bun run db:up`, migrations and every seed), so the 24 jobs, 24 notifications and 8 audit rows are gone and no deadline digest can reach Mailpit. Read-only check the same day (`BEGIN READ ONLY` as postgres on `sadar_bersuara`): 228 reports, 52 issues, 12 demands, 2 organizations, 91599 regions, 0 notifications with template referral_deadline, 0 audit_log rows `referral.remind`, 0 pgboss.job rows `email-send` in state created; roles sb_aggregates, sb_app, sb_backup, sb_media and sb_migrator exist, created fresh by infra/initdb.sql. Prevention, enforced since 22 October 2026: bunfig.toml `[test]` preloads db/test-env.ts, which sets NODE_ENV=test before any test module loads, and db/client.server.ts `appDatabaseUrl()` refuses a TEST_DATABASE_URL whose database name does not end in `_test` (db/client.test.ts "under test, the app pool refuses a non-test database" and "an inherited development NODE_ENV still runs tests as test").

Scratch databases left by interrupted audit agents after the reboot (`sadar_bersuara_gate_rel`, `sadar_bersuara_gate_top10`, `sadar_bersuara_gate_api2023`, `sadar_bersuara_cmp_test`, `sadar_bersuara_priv_test`) and the volume `sadar-bersuara-dev_pgdata_pre_sb` were dropped on 22 October 2026; the same read-only check lists only `sadar_bersuara` and `sadar_bersuara_test`, and `docker volume ls` only `sadar-bersuara-dev_pgdata`.

## Re-Audit

Start the dev services and migrate first:

```sh
bun run db:up && bun run db:migrate
```

Gates and tests that carry most of the evidence:

```sh
bun run typecheck && bun run lint && bun run test && bun run build && bun run size && bun run knip
shellcheck infra/*.sh
infra/osv-scan.sh lockfile bun.lock
docker build --target runtime --build-arg PGBOSS_VERSION=12.35.1 -f infra/Dockerfile -t sadar-bersuara:local .
docker build -f infra/postgres.Dockerfile -t sadar-bersuara-postgres:local infra
infra/stack-test.sh sadar-bersuara:local sadar-bersuara-postgres:local
infra/restore-test.sh
```

Backend API end to end (Playwright `request` API, no browser), against a disposable copy of the dev database, never the shared one ([ADR-23](decisions.md#adr-23-agentic-testing-pattern-for-the-backend-e2e-suite)):

```sh
# as postgres on 127.0.0.1:55432, with no other session on sadar_bersuara
CREATE DATABASE sadar_bersuara_e2e TEMPLATE sadar_bersuara OWNER sb_migrator;
GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE sadar_bersuara_e2e TO sb_app, sb_media, sb_backup;
ALTER DATABASE sadar_bersuara_e2e SET search_path = "$user", public, topology, tiger;
# export DATABASE_URL, DATABASE_MIGRATOR_URL, DATABASE_MEDIA_URL (the copy) and FILES_DIR (a scratch folder) in the shell, never in .env
bun run build
PORT=5198 NODE_ENV=production EMAIL_PROVIDER=brevo BREVO_API_KEY=unused bun --env-file=.env server/index.ts
SURVEY_ENABLED=false ERROR_REPORTING=false bun --env-file=.env run worker
bun --env-file=.env run worker:media
bun run e2e --project=api
# stop all three processes, then DROP DATABASE sadar_bersuara_e2e WITH (FORCE);
```

Result on 21 October 2026: `16 passed`, `4 skipped` (the survey, rating and right-of-reply scenarios wait for their routes), and `48 passed` with `--repeat-each=3`.

Standards: re-download the three sources in [Scope and Method](#scope-and-method), compare the hashes, and diff the requirement text before re-judging rows. Rows with `decision` are re-judged once the decision is recorded in decisions.md.

**30-row re-check sample.** After the fix batches, these `pass` rows were re-opened across chapters, weighted to the files the batches edited most (auth, app, security headers, logging, compose, Caddyfile, uploads). Each cited line, symbol and test name was read again in the current tree; rows whose citations had moved were rewritten (they carry "Re-checked against the tree"). Their auth citations were re-read again in review round 2 (Scope and Method, Line numbers). All 30 still pass:

V1.2.1, V1.3.10, V2.2.2, V2.4.1, V3.2.1, V3.3.1, V3.4.3, V3.5.4, V4.1.3, V4.2.1, V5.2.1, V5.4.2, V6.3.1, V6.4.2, V7.2.1, V7.4.1, V8.2.1, V8.3.1, V9.1.1, V9.1.3, V11.4.1, V11.6.1, V12.3.2, V13.2.2, V13.4.3, V14.2.1, V14.2.2, V15.3.4, V16.4.1, V16.5.1 (30 rows).

Findings of the re-check: the moved code was the cookie prefix (now `__Host-sb`, V3.3.1, V14.2.1, A02-CWE-1004/614/315, V3.3.5), the original-download headers (moved into `originalResponse`, V1.2.1, V3.2.1, V5.4.2, A05-CWE-93/113/644, A01-CWE-219/276/281/548/552/732/922), `/get-session` (no longer routed, V3.4.2, V3.5.3, V4.1.4), `/healthz/jobs` (detail behind a token, V16.5.1), unroutable paths (now logged, V16.4.1), the tuntutan prefix cap (now built, V2.4.1), and the install-script control (`trustedDependencies` now present, V15.2.4). Two rows the batches had not updated were corrected: V16.5.4 (fatal handlers now exist, the availability half stays open by design) and V6.5.6 (TOTP revocation exists; a proven WhatsApp number cannot be detached).

Known red test on 21 October 2026: `tooling.test.ts` "product name > backend says Sadar Bersuara only" fails on `.token-savior-cache.json:1`, a 4.5 MB local cache file of an editor tool in the repo root (ignored by a global gitignore, not part of the project). Deleting it, or making the test skip git-ignored files, turns the gate green.
